I don’t like saying that, but yes.\n\nDoctor: Have you had a period where you felt this way for a week or more straight, without a break, where you were really high, overly energetic, or irritable most of the time?\n\nPatient: Yes… I think so. It’s been going on, off and on but mostly there, for about two months now… since my brother passed and I started working on the tribute.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me more about how long these periods of feeling 'lit up' or irritable usually last?
